<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>Pet Bukvi — Word Solver</title>
<style>
  :root { color-scheme: dark; --bg:#111315; --panel:#1b1e21; --panel2:#24282c; --line:#373d42; --text:#f2f4f5; --muted:#a8b0b6; --green:#538d4e; --yellow:#b59f3b; --gray:#454b50; --accent:#8bb8ff; }
  * { box-sizing:border-box; }
  body { margin:0; background:var(--bg); color:var(--text); font:16px/1.45 system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,sans-serif; }
  main { width:min(1000px,100% - 32px); margin:32px auto 56px; }
  h1 { margin:0 0 4px; font-size:clamp(1.7rem,4vw,2.4rem); letter-spacing:-.035em; }
  h2 { margin:0 0 12px; font-size:1.15rem; }
  p { margin:.45rem 0; }
  .muted { color:var(--muted); }
  .panel { background:var(--panel); border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:14px; padding:20px; margin-top:18px; }
  .loadbox { border:1px dashed #59636c; background:var(--panel2); padding:18px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:center; gap:12px; }
  input[type=file] { max-width:100%; }
  input[type=text], input[type=search], select { background:#101214; color:var(--text); border:1px solid #525b63; border-radius:8px; padding:10px 12px; font:inherit; }
  button { font:inherit; color:var(--text); background:#30363b; border:1px solid #596168; border-radius:8px; padding:9px 13px; cursor:pointer; }
  button:hover:not(:disabled) { filter:brightness(1.15); }
  button:disabled { opacity:.45; cursor:not-allowed; }
  .primary { background:#315d9d; border-color:#477bc2; font-weight:650; }
  .toolbar { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; }
  .statsline { color:var(--muted); font-size:.94rem; }
  .guess-list { display:grid; gap:12px; margin:16px 0; }
  .guess-row { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; padding:12px; border:1px solid var(--line); border-radius:10px; background:#171a1c; }
  .guess-word { width:155px; text-transform:lowercase; letter-spacing:.06em; }
  .tiles { display:flex; gap:6px; }
  .tile { width:43px; height:43px; padding:0; display:grid; place-items:center; border:2px solid #596168; border-radius:5px; background:#111315; font-weight:750; font-size:1.15rem; text-transform:uppercase; }
  .tile.absent { background:var(--gray); border-color:var(--gray); }
  .tile.present { background:var(--yellow); border-color:var(--yellow); color:#fff; }
  .tile.correct { background:var(--green); border-color:var(--green); }
  .remove { margin-left:auto; padding:7px 10px; }
  .legend { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:12px; margin-top:12px; color:var(--muted); font-size:.9rem; }
  .key { display:inline-block; width:12px; height:12px; border-radius:3px; vertical-align:-1px; margin-right:5px; }
  .key.gray { background:var(--gray); } .key.yellow { background:var(--yellow); } .key.green { background:var(--green); }
  .actions { display:flex; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap; }
  #status { margin-top:12px; min-height:1.5em; }
  #status.error { color:#ff9999; } #status.success { color:#a8dfa3; }
  #results { margin-top:18px; }
  .result-head { display:flex; align-items:baseline; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-bottom:12px; }
  #result-count { font-size:1.4rem; font-weight:750; }
  .word-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(78px,1fr)); gap:7px; }
  .word-chip { text-align:center; padding:7px 5px; background:#252a2e; border:1px solid #41484e; border-radius:6px; font-weight:650; letter-spacing:.04em; }
  .note { font-size:.9rem; color:var(--muted); }
  @media (max-width:550px) { main { width:calc(100% - 20px); margin-top:18px; } .panel { padding:14px; } .guess-row { gap:8px; } .guess-word { width:100%; } .tile { width:min(15vw,43px); height:min(15vw,43px); } .remove { margin-left:0; } }
</style>
</head>
<body>
<main>
  <header>
    <h1>Pet Bukvi Word Solver</h1>
    <p class="muted">Import a JSON, TXT, or HTML word list, enter each guess and its tile colors, then filter possible answers.</p>
  </header>

  <section class="panel" aria-labelledby="dictionary-heading">
    <h2 id="dictionary-heading">1. Load a dictionary</h2>
    <div class="loadbox" id="dropzone">
      <label for="file">Choose a dictionary file</label>
      <input id="file" type="file" accept=".json,.txt,.html,.htm,application/json,text/plain,text/html">
      <button id="use-integrated" type="button">Use integrated dictionary</button>
      <span id="file-status" class="statsline">No dictionary loaded.</span>
    </div>
    <div class="toolbar" style="margin-top:14px">
      <label for="pool">Candidate pool</label>
      <select id="pool" disabled>
        <option value="answers">Possible answers</option>
        <option value="accepted">All accepted words</option>
      </select>
    </div>
    <p class="note">Supports JSON files, newline-separated <code>.txt</code> word lists, and HTML pages containing word lists. A plain list is used for both candidate pools because it does not distinguish answers from accepted guesses.</p>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" aria-labelledby="guesses-heading">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <h2 id="guesses-heading">2. Enter your guesses and feedback</h2>
      <button id="add-guess" type="button">+ Add guess</button>
    </div>
    <p class="note">Type the five-letter word you guessed. Click each tile until its color matches the game: unknown → gray → yellow → green. Set all five tiles for every guess.</p>
    <div class="legend" aria-label="Tile color legend">
      <span><i class="key gray"></i>Gray: absent</span>
      <span><i class="key yellow"></i>Yellow: present, wrong position</span>
      <span><i class="key green"></i>Green: correct position</span>
    </div>
    <div id="guess-list" class="guess-list"></div>
    <div class="actions">
      <button id="solve" class="primary" type="button">Find possible answers</button>
      <button id="clear" type="button">Clear guesses</button>
    </div>
    <p id="status" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>
  </section>

  <section id="results" class="panel" aria-labelledby="results-heading" hidden>
    <div class="toolbar">
      <div>
        <h2 id="results-heading">Possible answers</h2>
        <div class="result-head"><span id="result-count">0</span><span class="muted">matching words</span></div>
      </div>
      <button id="download-results" type="button">Download candidates (.txt)</button>
    </div>
    <div id="result-words" class="word-grid"></div>
    <p id="display-note" class="note"></p>
  </section>
</main>

    "ядачо",
    "ядвай",
    "ядвал",
    "ядвам",
    "ядван",
    "ядват",
    "ядвах",
    "ядваш",
    "ядващ",
    "ядели",
    "ядена",
    "ядене",
    "ядени",
    "ядено",
    "ядете",
    "ядеца",
    "ядеше",
    "ядива",

    "ядков",
    "ядлив",
    "ядния",
    "ядове",
    "ядоса",

    "ядрен",
    "ядяла",
    "ядяло",
    "ядяха",
    "ядяща",
    "ядящи",
    "ядящо",
    "язвен",
    "яздел",
    "язден",
    "яздех",
    "яздещ",
    "яздил",
    "яздим",
    "яздих",
    "яздиш",
    "яздят",
    "язове",
    "яйчар",
    "яйчев",
    "яйчен",
    "яйчна",
    "яйчни",
    "яйчно",
    "якала",
    "якали",
    "якало",
    "якаме",
    "якане",
    "яката",
    "якате",
    "якаха",
    "якаше",
    "якаща",
    "якащи",
    "якащо",
    "якета",
    "якето",
    "яките",
    "якият",
    "якове",
    "якост",
    "якото",
    "ялата",
    "ялите",
    "ялият",
    "ялова",
    "ялови",
    "ялово",
    "ялото",
    "ямаец",
    "ямата",
    "ямаха",
    "ямахи",
    "ямбче",
    "ямбът",
    "ямите",
    "янтар",
    "ярдът",
    "ярема",
    "яреми",
    "ярета",
    "ярето",
    "ярина",
    "ярини",
    "яркия",
    "ярост",
    "ясена",
    "ясени",
    "яслен",
    "ясмин",
    "яснее",
    "ясней",
    "яснея",
    "ясния",
    "яснял",
    "яснях",
    "яспис",
    "ястие",
    "ястия",
    "ятака",
    "ятако",
    "ятата",
    "ятаци",
    "ятова",
    "ятови",
    "ятово",
    "ятото",
    "яхала",
    "яхали",
    "яхало",
    "яхаме",
    "яхана",
    "яхане",
    "яхани",
    "яхано",
    "яхате",
    "яхаха",
    "яхаше",
    "яхаща",
    "яхащи",
    "яхащо",
    "яхвай",
    "яхвал",
    "яхвам",
    "яхван",
    "яхват",
    "яхвах",
    "яхваш",
    "яхващ",
    "яхнал",
    "яхнат",
    "яхнах",
    "яхнел",
    "яхнем",
    "яхнех",
    "яхнеш",
    "яхнии",
    "яхния",
    "яхтен",
    "яхъра",
    "яхъри",
    "ячкия",
    "ячмяк",
    "яшмак",
    "яшния"
  ]
};

  let lexicon = null;
  let guesses = [];
  let candidates = [];
  const stateNames = { unknown: 'Not set', absent: 'Gray — absent', present: 'Yellow — present in another position', correct: 'Green — correct position' };
  const stateCycle = ['unknown', 'absent', 'present', 'correct'];

  function normalizeWord(value) {
    return String(value ?? '').normalize('NFC').toLocaleLowerCase('bg').replace(/[^а-яѝ]/g, '').slice(0, 5);
  }
  function cleanWords(value) {
    if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
    // Require exactly five Bulgarian letters; do not truncate longer entries.
    return [...new Set(value.map(item => String(item ?? '').normalize('NFC').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('bg'))
      .filter(word => /^[а-яѝ]{5}$/u.test(word)))];
  }
  function setStatus(message, type = '') {
    $('status').textContent = message;
    $('status').className = type;
  }
  function invalidateResults() {
    $('results').hidden = true;
    candidates = [];
  }
  function loadLexicon(data, fileName = 'JSON dictionary') {
    let answers = [], accepted = [];
    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
      answers = cleanWords(data);
      accepted = answers;
    } else if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
      answers = cleanWords(data.answers);
      accepted = cleanWords(data.accepted);
      if (!answers.length && accepted.length) answers = accepted;
      if (!accepted.length && answers.length) accepted = answers;
    }
    if (!answers.length && !accepted.length) {
      throw new Error('No five-letter Bulgarian words found. Expected an answers or accepted array.');
    }
    lexicon = { answers, accepted };
    $('pool').disabled = false;
    $('pool').value = answers.length ? 'answers' : 'accepted';
    if (!answers.length) $('pool').value = 'accepted';
    $('pool').querySelector('[value="answers"]').disabled = !answers.length;
    $('pool').querySelector('[value="accepted"]').disabled = !accepted.length;
    $('file-status').textContent = `${fileName}: ${answers.length.toLocaleString('bg')} answers, ${accepted.length.toLocaleString('bg')} accepted words`;
    invalidateResults();
    setStatus('Dictionary loaded. Enter your guesses and feedback.', 'success');
  }
  function parseWordList(text, fileName) {
    let source = String(text ?? '');
    const looksLikeHTML = /\.html?$/i.test(fileName) || /<html[\s>]|<body[\s>]/i.test(source);
    if (looksLikeHTML) {
      const parsed = new DOMParser().parseFromString(source, 'text/html');
      parsed.querySelectorAll('script,style,noscript,template').forEach(element => element.remove());
      parsed.querySelectorAll('br').forEach(element => element.replaceWith('\n'));
      parsed.querySelectorAll('li,p,div,pre,section,article,tr,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6').forEach(element => element.append('\n'));
      source = parsed.body.textContent || '';
    }
    const words = source.normalize('NFC').split(/[\s,;]+/u)
      .map(token => token.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('bg').replace(/^[^а-яѝ]+|[^а-яѝ]+$/gu, ''))
      .filter(token => /^[а-яѝ]{5}$/u.test(token));
    const unique = [...new Set(words)];
    if (!unique.length) throw new Error('No five-letter Bulgarian words found in this text or HTML file.');
    loadLexicon(unique, fileName + (looksLikeHTML ? ' (HTML word list)' : ' (text word list)'));
    setStatus(`Loaded ${unique.length.toLocaleString('bg')} words from a plain list. The same list is used for both candidate pools.`, 'success');
  }

  async function readFile(file) {
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const raw = await file.text();
      const extension = file.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
      const mightBeJSON = extension === 'json' || /^[\s\uFEFF]*[\[{]/u.test(raw);
      if (mightBeJSON) {
        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(raw.replace(/^\uFEFF/u, ''));
          loadLexicon(data, file.name);
          return;
        } catch (error) {
          if (extension === 'json') throw new Error('Invalid JSON file: ' + error.message);
          // A non-JSON file can still contain a word list; parse it as text below.
        }
      }
      parseWordList(raw, file.name);
    } catch (error) {
      $('file-status').textContent = 'Could not load dictionary.';
      setStatus(error.message || 'Could not load dictionary.', 'error');
    }
  }

  function useIntegratedDictionary() {
    if (EMBEDDED_DICTIONARY === null) {
      setStatus('No integrated dictionary is configured yet. Paste your JSON into EMBEDDED_DICTIONARY near the top of the script, replacing null.', 'error');
      return;
    }
    try {
      loadLexicon(EMBEDDED_DICTIONARY, 'Integrated dictionary');
    } catch (error) {
      $('file-status').textContent = 'Could not load integrated dictionary.';
      setStatus(error.message || 'Could not load integrated dictionary.', 'error');
    }
  }

  function updateTile(tile, row, index) {
    const state = row.pattern[index];
    tile.className = 'tile' + (state === 'unknown' ? '' : ' ' + state);
    tile.textContent = row.guess[index] || '·';
    const letter = row.guess[index] ? row.guess[index].toLocaleUpperCase('bg') : 'empty';
    tile.title = `${letter}: ${stateNames[state]}`;
    tile.setAttribute('aria-label', `${letter}: ${stateNames[state]}. Click to change color.`);
  }
  function addGuess(initial = '') {
    const row = { guess: normalizeWord(initial), pattern: Array(5).fill('unknown'), element: null };
    const container = document.createElement('div');
    container.className = 'guess-row';

    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.className = 'guess-word';
    input.type = 'text'; input.maxLength = 5; input.autocomplete = 'off';
    input.autocapitalize = 'off'; input.spellcheck = false; input.lang = 'bg';
    input.placeholder = 'петбуквена дума'; input.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Your five-letter guess');
    input.value = row.guess;
    const tiles = document.createElement('div'); tiles.className = 'tiles';
    const tileButtons = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const tile = document.createElement('button');
      tile.type = 'button'; tile.className = 'tile';
      tile.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const current = stateCycle.indexOf(row.pattern[i]);
        row.pattern[i] = stateCycle[(current + 1) % stateCycle.length];
        updateTile(tile, row, i);
        invalidateResults();
      });
      tileButtons.push(tile); tiles.append(tile);
      updateTile(tile, row, i);
    }
    input.addEventListener('input', () => {
      const cleaned = normalizeWord(input.value);
      row.guess = cleaned;
      if (input.value !== cleaned) input.value = cleaned;
      tileButtons.forEach((tile, i) => updateTile(tile, row, i));
      invalidateResults();
    });
    const remove = document.createElement('button');
    remove.type = 'button'; remove.className = 'remove'; remove.textContent = 'Remove';
    remove.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Remove this guess');
    remove.addEventListener('click', () => {
      guesses = guesses.filter(item => item !== row);
      container.remove(); invalidateResults();
      setStatus('Guess removed. Solve again to update candidates.');
    });
    container.append(input, tiles, remove);
    row.element = container;
    guesses.push(row); $('guess-list').append(container);
    invalidateResults();
    setStatus('');
  }

  // Reproduces Wordle-style scoring, including duplicate-letter counts.
  function scoreGuess(guess, answer) {
    const g = [...guess], a = [...answer];
    const result = Array(5).fill('absent');
    const remaining = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      if (g[i] === a[i]) result[i] = 'correct';
      else remaining.set(a[i], (remaining.get(a[i]) || 0) + 1);
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      if (result[i] === 'correct') continue;
      const n = remaining.get(g[i]) || 0;
      if (n > 0) {
        result[i] = 'present';
        remaining.set(g[i], n - 1);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
  function solve() {
    if (!lexicon) {
      setStatus('Load a dictionary first.', 'error'); return;
    }
    if (!guesses.length) {
      setStatus('Add at least one guess and set its tile feedback.', 'error'); return;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < guesses.length; i++) {
      const row = guesses[i];
      if ([...row.guess].length !== 5) {
        setStatus(`Guess ${i + 1} must contain exactly five Bulgarian letters.`, 'error'); return;
      }
      if (row.pattern.some(state => state === 'unknown')) {
        setStatus(`Set all five tile colors for guess ${i + 1}. Click each tile to cycle through the colors.`, 'error'); return;
      }
    }
    const pool = lexicon[$('pool').value] || lexicon.answers;
    const matching = pool.filter(answer => guesses.every(row => {
      const actual = scoreGuess(row.guess, answer);
      return actual.every((state, i) => state === row.pattern[i]);
    })).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'bg'));
    candidates = matching;
    $('results').hidden = false;
    $('result-count').textContent = matching.length.toLocaleString('bg');
    const list = $('result-words'); list.replaceChildren();
    const maxDisplay = 1500;
    const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const word of matching.slice(0, maxDisplay)) {
      const chip = document.createElement('div'); chip.className = 'word-chip';
      chip.textContent = word.toLocaleUpperCase('bg');
      fragment.append(chip);
    }
    list.append(fragment);
    $('display-note').textContent = matching.length > maxDisplay
      ? `Showing the first ${maxDisplay.toLocaleString('bg')} words here. Download candidates to get the complete list.`
      : matching.length === 0
        ? 'No matches. Check that each guess and all five feedback colors match the game.'
        : 'Candidates are sorted alphabetically. Export them to use the list elsewhere.';
    setStatus(matching.length ? `Found ${matching.length.toLocaleString('bg')} matching ${matching.length === 1 ? 'word' : 'words'}.` : 'No candidates match the feedback.', matching.length ? 'success' : 'error');
  }
  function downloadCandidates() {
    const blob = new Blob([candidates.join('\n') + (candidates.length ? '\n' : '')], {type:'text/plain;charset=utf-8'});
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a'); link.href = url; link.download = 'pet-bukvi-candidates.txt';
    document.body.append(link); link.click(); link.remove(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
  }

  $('file').addEventListener('change', event => readFile(event.target.files?.[0]));
  $('use-integrated').addEventListener('click', useIntegratedDictionary);
  $('pool').addEventListener('change', () => { invalidateResults(); setStatus('Candidate pool changed. Solve again to refresh results.'); });
  $('add-guess').addEventListener('click', () => addGuess());
  $('solve').addEventListener('click', solve);
  $('clear').addEventListener('click', () => {
    guesses = []; $('guess-list').replaceChildren(); addGuess(); setStatus('Guesses cleared.');
  });
  $('download-results').addEventListener('click', downloadCandidates);
  const dropzone = $('dropzone');
  dropzone.addEventListener('dragover', event => { event.preventDefault(); dropzone.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)'; });
  dropzone.addEventListener('dragleave', () => { dropzone.style.borderColor = ''; });
  dropzone.addEventListener('drop', event => {
    event.preventDefault(); dropzone.style.borderColor = '';
    const file = [...(event.dataTransfer?.files || [])].find(f => /\.(json|txt|html?)$/i.test(f.name) || /^(application\/json|text\/(plain|html))$/i.test(f.type));
    if (file) readFile(file); else setStatus('Drop a JSON, TXT, or HTML dictionary file.', 'error');
  });
  addGuess();
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
